【臺灣氣象部門釋出颱風“薔琵”海上警報】臺灣地區氣象部門21日23時30分針對臺風“薔琵”釋出海上颱風警報，颱風中心目前在臺灣南部的鵝鑾鼻東南東方海面，向西北移動，對巴士海峽及臺灣東半部海面構成威脅。據預測，23日至24日將是颱風對臺灣影響較明顯的時段。受颱風外圍環流與東北季風共同影響，臺灣北部、東北部、宜蘭及花蓮地區需防範區域性大雨或豪雨，北部山區不排除出現大豪雨等級降雨。氣象部門提醒，民眾應持續關注最新臺風路徑及警報資訊，避免前往山區、溪流及海岸等高風險區域，提前做好防颱準備。（央視新聞）

### 2026-10-22 02:35:34  #國際

【報道：美墨貿易談判卡在鋼鋁關稅，盧特尼克企圖擴大自己的影響力】據知情人士透露，美國和墨西哥的貿易談判因鋼鋁關稅問題陷入僵局，令此前有關協議即將達成的樂觀預期落空，重大進展可能要等到中期選舉之後。近幾日談判已經降溫，包括美國商務部長盧特尼克在內的美方官員對墨西哥要求下調金屬關稅表示不滿。美墨談判之前由美國貿易代表格里爾主導，盧特尼克近期試圖擴大自己在該談判以及整體貿易政策中的影響力。盧特尼克提出的方案未能解決墨方關切，隨後兩國談判陷入停滯。如今，美方官員已不排斥協議可能要到11月3日中期選舉之後較長一段時間才能達成。

### 2026-10-22 02:14:57  #MKT News #Star Stocks #NVIDIA #Market Themes #Magnificent 7 #XAUUSD #Impact bullish #DXY #S&P500 #Impact bearish

【SpaceX beats estimates but sparks debt concerns; credit risk metrics continue to rise】SpaceX reported Q3 net income of $4.4 billion versus $1.88 billion expected and revenue up 161% YoY, sending shares as much as 12.4% higher intraday on Wednesday. Despite the beat, the company said it will continue heavy investment, prompting market concern over larger future borrowing. The 6.6% bond due 2046 widened 9bps to a 264bps spread; the five-year CDS rose to 232.6bps, marking multiple fresh highs this month. CFO Brett Johnson said SpaceX will prioritize compute capacity for its super-intelligence unit and investment in Starlink broadband and mobile, and is in talks with multiple debt providers. The company issued $25 billion of bonds in June and has been reported to be seeking about $40 billion of financing to buy NVIDIA chips. Johnson said SpaceX plans to maintain an investment-grade rating.

### 2026-10-22 00:22:29  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Gasoline Slips Back to Seven-Week Low — US gasoline futures fell to around $3.10 per gallon, reaching a seven-week low as hopes of Russia-Ukraine diplomatic negotiations offset concerns over a persistently tight fuel market. Russia has agreed to resume trilateral talks with the US and Ukraine on a potential peace deal which could increase fuel supplies in global markets. Russia also announced that its first diesel cargo of around 300,000 barrels destined for the US would depart on Thursday, although its market impact could be limited as the US diesel exports about 1.5 million barrels a day. Meanwhile, the US Energy Department announced plans to loan 315,000 barrels from the 1 million-barrel Northeast Home Heating Oil Reserve. However, EIA data showed US gasoline inventories fell nearly 3 million barrels in the week ending October 16th, reversing prior builds and adding to warnings of a persistently tight fuel market. Morgan Stanley has warned they could remain tight through mid-2027 as planned refinery outages rise sharply.

French Stocks Rebound as Earnings Roll In — The CAC 40 rose 1.1% to close at 7,922 on Wednesday, rebounding after two consecutive sessions of losses as corporate earnings roll in. EssilorLuxottica soared 15.5% after beating revenue expectations on resilient eyewear demand, recovering from Tuesday’s decline amid weaker sales of digital eyewear. Hermès (+1.3%) and L’Oréal (+0.4%) advanced ahead of their earnings reports due Thursday. In contrast, Michelin slumped 7.2% after reporting lower nine-month sales amid declining tire volumes. Easing oil prices offered some relief to financial stocks, which have faced pressure from energy-driven inflation concerns and expectations of higher-for-longer interest rates. AXA and Société Générale each gained 0.8%. Technology stocks also advanced, with STMicroelectronics up 2.5% and Schneider Electric rising 2%, tracking broader sector strength after solid earnings from major US tech companies. Meanwhile, France’s uncertain fiscal and political outlook remained a concern.

Italian Stocks Rebound from Five-Month Low — Italy’s FTSE MIB closed 1.2% higher at 49,643 on Wednesday, rebounding from a five-month low, lifted by gains in tech and heavyweight financials. Prysmian (2.8%), Technoprobe (2.8%) and STMicroelectronics (2.6%) advanced, tracking optimism across the sector following upbeat results from SpaceX and Super Micro Computer. Financials also outperformed while discussions on the sector’s contribution to Italy’s public finances were postponed until next week. Intesa rose 1.3% aid reports that it's working on significant risk transfers to hedge more than €5 billion in assets, despite concerns over debt and geopolitical tensions. Energy shares gained on a rebound in oil prices, led by Saipem (1.8%) and ENI (1.8%). Utilities also advanced, with Snam rising 1.2%. Stellantis dropped 0.4% amid increased short positions by asset managers. Defense stocks declined as trilateral talks involving the US, Russia and Ukraine on a potential peace deal resumed, with Leonardo dropping 2% to lead losses.

FTSE 100 Edges Up on Wednesday — The FTSE 100 edged higher on Wednesday, recovering from two sessions of losses but underperforming other European markets. Oil majors provided support, with BP gaining around 2%, while HSBC, Lloyds Banking Group, NatWest and Standard Chartered advanced between 0.5% and 1.1%. Barclays slipped slightly ahead of its corporate results due on Thursday. Fresnillo fell more than 2% after maintaining its annual silver production outlook despite lower output compared with last year. Investors also assessed fresh UK economic data showing persistent inflationary pressure and rising public borrowing. Annual consumer price inflation accelerated to 3.5% in September, above expectations of 3.4%, potentially strengthening the case for tighter monetary policy from the Bank of England. Meanwhile, government borrowing reached £97.8 billion in the April–September period, exceeding the Office for Budget Responsibility’s March forecast by £6 billion, while September borrowing totalled £24.5 billion.

### 2026-10-21 23:48:25  #Trading Economics #Economy #Colombia #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Colombia Trade Deficit Widens in August — Colombia’s trade deficit widened to $3.25 billion in August 2026, from $2.10 billion a year earlier. Imports rose 31.7% year-on-year to $7.70 billion, led by manufactured goods, with purchases of machinery and transport equipment increasing 22.8% and chemicals and related products rising 22.2%. Imports of fuels and extractive industry products surged 190.1%, driven by a 262.6% jump in purchases of petroleum, petroleum products, and related materials. Imports of agricultural products, food, and beverages rose 24.8%. Exports increased 15.1% year-on-year to $4.46 billion, mainly driven by a 127% surge in the other sectors category, reflecting higher non-monetary gold sales. Exports of fuels and extractive industry products rose 23.8%, led by increases in coal, coke, and briquettes (99.4%) and petroleum products (12.3%). Manufacturing exports grew 5.6%, supported by chemicals and related products (18.7%) and miscellaneous manufactured articles (1.9%).

【Putin agrees in principle to resume Russia‑US‑Ukraine trilateral talks; Abu Dhabi possible】The Kremlin said President Putin told a U.S. special envoy on Wednesday he "agreed in principle" to resume trilateral peace talks among Russia, Ukraine and the United States around Nov. 10, possibly in Abu Dhabi, Reuters and Axios reported. The envoy held about three hours of talks with Putin in St. Petersburg and delivered an updated proposal to end the conflict; former Trump adviser Jared Kushner joined by video. The envoy said progress at Oslo talks supports restarting trilateral negotiations and involving technical experts on specific issues, and handed Putin several documents on a peaceful settlement. Putin also proposed discussing a potential Russian mediation role between the U.S. and Iran, citing decades of mutual trust and offering assistance to resolve the current crisis.

European Stocks Rebound on Wednesday — European stocks rebounded on Wednesday after two consecutive sessions of losses, with the Euro Stoxx 50 rising more than 1% and the broader Stoxx 600 gaining less than 1%. Germany, France, Italy and Spain all advanced by over 1%, while the UK market posted a more modest recovery. Technology shares led sector gains, climbing around 2%, followed by energy stocks, which rose more than 1%. Banking, industrial goods and services, healthcare, insurance and utilities also recorded gains of approximately 1%. Automobiles and parts was the weakest-performing sector, falling more than 1.5%. Among individual stocks, EssilorLuxottica surged over 15% after reporting third-quarter revenue that exceeded market expectations. Carrefour, by contrast, plunged more than 10% after cutting its profit outlook and reducing planned capital expenditure. The French retailer cited continued domestic unrest and an energy price shock as factors weighing on consumer confidence and complicating its business outlook.

Colombia Imports Rise the Most in 4 Years — Colombia's imports surged 31.7% year-on-year to $7.7 billion in August 2026, marking the strongest increase since August 2022, following a 22.9% rise in July. Imports of fuels and extractive industry products jumped 190.1% after increasing 77.3% in the previous month, driven by a sharp 262.6% rise in purchases of oil, petroleum products and related goods. Import growth in manufactured goods also accelerated to 18.9% from 16.1%, supported by stronger purchases of machinery and transport equipment (22.8%) and chemical products (22.2%). Meanwhile, imports of agricultural and food products continued to grow, although at a slower pace of 24.8% compared to 27.2% in July.

### 2026-10-21 23:10:23  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

US Heating Oil Falls as Government Releases Emergency Supplies — Heating oil prices in the US fell toward $4.80 a gallon as the Trump administration moved to release emergency fuel supplies to ease rising costs ahead of the midterm elections. The Energy Department announced plans to loan 315,000 barrels from the 1 million-barrel Northeast Home Heating Oil Reserve, with deliveries expected to be completed by November 13. Energy Secretary Chris Wright described the move as a temporary response to supply disruptions. Around 4% of US households rely on heating oil, particularly in northeastern states, although the release is unlikely to materially affect the broader diesel market, given that the East Coast consumes approximately 1.2 million barrels of diesel and other distillates daily. Established under President Bill Clinton, the reserve is designed to provide the region with roughly 10 days of supply and was previously used following Hurricane Sandy and major snowstorms in 2012.

【聯合國環境署：全球減排有成效但力度仍不足】聯合國環境規劃署21日釋出的《2026年排放差距報告》顯示，發展可再生能源和實施氣候政策使各國在減排方面取得切實成效，但現行政策和減排承諾仍不足以實現《巴黎協定》提出的控溫目標，各方需進一步加快行動。《巴黎協定》提出，到本世紀末，應將全球平均氣溫較工業化前水平升高幅度控制在2攝氏度之內，併為把升溫幅度控制在1.5攝氏度內而努力。這份報告說，2016年至2025年，全球有63個國家或地區的溫室氣體年排放量下降，同期有90個國家或地區電力行業排放下降。研究發現，2000年至2020年間，各國或各相關行業發生過69次大幅減排，其中63次發生在新出臺或強化氣候相關政策的2年內。（新華社）

### 2026-10-21 22:30:39  #其他

【紡織品正在上天入海進人體 我國化纖產量佔全球75%以上】紡織是個老產業，但它今天的下游，早已不只是服裝廠。中國工程院院士陳文興向央視財經記者介紹，紡織品正在上天下海進人體。上天，國產大飛機、導彈、火箭都要用到碳纖維複合材料，神舟飛船回收用到了超高分子量聚乙烯纖維；下海，滌綸工業絲做的纜繩，一根纜繩可以承重2000噸；進人體，人工血管、心臟瓣膜、疝氣補片、人工韌帶、可吸收縫合線等，都是紡織產品。這些應用背後，是高效能的纖維材料，更是生產纖維的高階裝備。高階電子布的織機、噴氣渦流紡紗機、數碼印花的噴頭，部分還被國外壟斷。這個卡脖子的問題，過去我們跟跑，現在領跑並跑，靠的是自主創新、原始創新。從材料、加工、裝備到產品應用，整條鏈一起上。這樣的協同攻關，成效正在顯現。碳纖維、芳綸、超高分子量聚乙烯等關鍵戰略材料相繼實現產業化，國外壟斷正在被逐一打破。我們國家已經基本實現紡織強國目標，國產的紡織裝備，自給率已經達到了85%以上，我國的化纖產量佔到全球的75%以上（央視財經）

Baltic Dry Index Up for 3rd Day — The Baltic Exchange’s dry bulk freight index, which tracks rates for ships carrying dry bulk commodities, advanced for a third session on Wednesday, rising by 3.4% to its highest since October 13 at 2,680 points. Strength in the bigger-size segment continued to provide the main support. The capesize index, which typically transports 150,000-ton cargoes, including iron ore and coal, increased for a third day, surging by 7.7% to 3,793 points. At the same time, the supramax index rose by 0.1% to 1,870 points. Conversely, the panamax index, which usually carries 60,000 to 70,000 tons of coal or grain, fell by 1.9% to 2,204 points.

Ibovespa Slips as Poll Shows Tight Presidential Race — The Ibovespa edged lower toward the 208,000 mark on Wednesday following a new poll on the presidential runoff between Senator Flávio Bolsonaro and President Lula. A Quaest poll showed a one-percentage-point gap between the candidates, with Flávio Bolsonaro at 45% and Lula at 44%. Given the margin of error of two percentage points, the race remains statistically tied. Compared with last week’s poll, however, Flávio’s support slipped by one percentage point. Although Bolsonaro has yet to detail his policy plans, investors view him as more likely to bring fiscal reforms that could help lower interest rates. Financial stocks were mixed, with Itaú and Bradesco edging higher, while Itaúsa and Caixa fell more than 0.5%. BB Seguridade gained nearly 0.5%, and B3 rose more than 1%. Elsewhere, WEG plunged nearly 5% after reporting net income below expectations. Vale shed more than 1% amid falling iron ore prices, while Petrobras edged lower as oil prices remained volatile.

US Stocks Trade Mixed, With Tech Shares Lifting Nasdaq — US stocks showed a mixed performance on Wednesday, with the S&P 500 trading flat, the Dow Jones slipping 60 points, and the Nasdaq advancing 0.6%, supported by upbeat results from SpaceX and Super Micro Computer that revived optimism around AI demand. SpaceX climbed 9% after Elon Musk said a merger with Tesla would be “a good idea,” while the space technology company reported its first profit as a public company. Tesla gained 2.5%, while other space stocks like Rocket Lab, Planet Labs and Intuitive Machines rose between 3.9% and 6.5%. Super Micro surged 14% after issuing a strong quarterly outlook, lifting HPE and Dell by 4.5% and 7.7%, respectively. Most chipmakers also rose, with Nvidia, Intel and AMD leading the gains. Meanwhile, Netflix fell more than 4% after issuing a weaker-than-expected Q4 profit forecast, days after announcing workforce cuts. AT&T dropped 3.5% despite beating earnings estimates, while Elevance Health fell 1.2% after raising its 2026 profit outlook.

### 2026-10-21 21:29:15  #公司

【地平線創始人兼CEO餘凱：智駕現在是可用，距離好用還要跨越幾次“太平洋”】地平線創始人兼CEO餘凱21日在2026世界智慧網聯汽車大會上指出，智駕現在是可用，距離好用還要跨越幾次“太平洋”。“智駕下半場”需要考慮的問題有：MPCI（每關鍵接管行駛里程）如何從百公里跨越到1000公里？所有的技術問題是否都解決了？跨越1000公里以後朝著1萬公里走的時候，是不是又沿著那個延長線再往前走？他認為，對於這些問題，既要樂觀，也要看到這個事情本身還很難，還要跨越好幾次太平洋。每一次的10倍量級的 MPCI 里程的躍遷，就是跨越一次太平洋，可能還要跨越若干次太平洋才能真正達到我們所希望的。他覺得現在的智駕是可用，下一個階段MPCI 到1000公里、10000公里是好用，那真正到愛用可能要10萬公里量級。“希望在未來的4到5年裡，我們能跨越過去。”（國是直通車）

### 2026-10-21 21:25:51  #公司 #資料 #市場

【谷歌CEO：幾乎所有企業都面臨算力短缺 優先保障前沿研發算力供給】桑達爾·皮查伊表示：“我們第一優先順序，是保障前沿AI研發所需的算力投入。2015年我確立了谷歌AI優先戰略，AI是貫穿搜尋、雲業務、Waymo所有業務的底層核心。保障研發、訓練模型的算力供給，是我們的根基。 在此基礎上，各個獨立業務線擁有各自長期規劃，基於預測來分配算力資源。雲團隊有自身業務目標、需求訊號以及已簽署的長期合約，我們以此規劃雲業務長期算力供給。”他表示， 現在，幾乎所有企業都面臨算力短缺，大家都希望手裡的算力是原定規劃的兩倍，這是行業常態。我們現在就要預判兩到三年後的算力需求。有些兩年前定下的決策，放到新的技術環境下看或許不算最優，但我不會過度糾結這件事。 他表示，他的算力分配邏輯是優先保障Google DeepMind的前沿研發算力供給，在此之後，再為各個業務部門規劃資源。

### 2026-10-21 21:22:47  #Trading Economics #Economy #Israel #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Bank of Israel Holds Key Rate at 3.25% — The Bank of Israel left its benchmark interest rate unchanged at 3.25% at its latest meeting, its lowest level since 2022, following three consecutive rate cuts. Annual inflation stood at 1.6%, below the midpoint of the 1%-3% target range. Policymakers noted that inflation moderated, helped by a strong shekel against the dollar, and expected it to remain around the midpoint of the target range. They also pointed to high uncertainty amid geopolitical tensions, although the risk premium remained broadly stable, and to economic activity that appeared to have slowed at the start of Q3, after expanding 15.4% annualized in Q2. Future decisions will focus on price stability, while providing support for economic activity.

### 2026-10-21 21:17:58  #Trading Economics #Markets #South Africa #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 0

South Africa 10-Year Bond Yield at 1-Month Low — South Africa’s 10-year government bond yield eased to below 8.80%, its lowest since September 22, as investors welcomed the Treasury’s medium-term budget and its renewed commitment to fiscal discipline. The budget pointed to a narrowing deficit and a stabilizing debt-to-GDP ratio, despite weaker growth. Importantly, Finance Minister Enoch Godongwana unveiled draft legislation for public consultation to establish a principles-based fiscal anchor, which is expected to be submitted to Parliament in 2027. The proposed framework aims to prevent a repeat of the fiscal deterioration seen between the global financial crisis and the early 2020s. Meanwhile, the government also extended fuel price relief for two months to cushion from the impact of global oil prices. Data released earlier showed South Africa's inflation rate rose further to 4.5% in September from 4.4% in August, though slightly below the expected 4.7%, mainly due to higher domestic fuel prices.

Silver Slips on Rate-Hike Fears — Silver prices stayed below $58 an ounce on Wednesday as elevated oil prices heightened inflation concerns ahead of next week’s Federal Reserve meeting. The metal has faced sustained pressure in recent weeks as expectations of further monetary tightening weighed on demand for non-yielding assets. Markets expect the Fed to hold interest rates steady on October 28, while pricing in a potential hike in December amid persistent price pressures. Investors are also awaiting next week’s US PCE inflation report, the Fed’s preferred measure of inflation. Recent stronger-than-expected US consumer and producer inflation data have reinforced concerns that rising energy costs could keep inflation elevated. Meanwhile, UK inflation climbed to an 11-month high of 3.5% in September, strengthening expectations that the Bank of England could raise interest rates as soon as November.

Brent Falls Below $108 — Brent crude reversed earlier gains to trade below $108 a barrel on Wednesday, as relatively strong flows through the Strait of Hormuz offset concerns over escalating attacks on shipping and energy infrastructure across the Middle East. Iran’s Revolutionary Guards said naval mines had halted three tankers attempting to use routes Tehran considered unauthorized, while the UK Maritime Trade Operations agency reported a vessel had been struck near Oman. Despite the security risks, US Secretary of State Marco Rubio said shipments through the chokepoint were approaching prewar levels, while data from Kpler and Vortexa showed broader Middle Eastern exports had risen since the summer. Separately, Yemen’s Houthi militants claimed attacks on Saudi Aramco facilities in Yanbu and the Shaybah oilfield, as well as airports in Riyadh and Abha. Investors awaited US inventory data after the American Petroleum Institute reported a 4.9 million-barrel decline in crude stocks last week.

【中金公司董事總經理張一鳴：中國AI產業趨勢沒有改變】“AI產業趨勢沒有改變，變的是市場對定價邏輯的校準。”21日，中金公司董事總經理、全球股票業務管理委員會主任張一鳴在2026金融街論壇年會“共建服務中小企業資本市場新生態”分論壇期間接受中新經緯專訪時表示。張一鳴表示，上半年AI產業鏈部分環節漲幅較快、交易較為擁擠，當海外AI資本開支節奏和模型商業化進展出現階段性波動時，估值向基本面迴歸是市場機制的正常發揮。這種校準長期將更有助於真正具備技術壁壘和訂單兌現能力的優質企業。他同時提到，如果市場把具備業績支撐的龍頭標的的估值也一併歸為“泡沫”，顯然忽視了中國AI產業鏈的堅實基本面。以光模組、算力基礎設施為代表的核心環節，1.6T高速光模組產品放量節奏明確，龍頭公司營收、利潤增速持續驗證產業景氣度。AI產業鏈內部將走向分化，具備全球競爭力的龍頭公司在估值消化後仍具備中長期配置價值。（中新經緯）

South African Rand Hovers at 1-Month High — The South African rand hovered around 16.4 per USD, close to its highest level since September 23, after Finance Minister Enoch Godongwana unveiled the medium-term budget statement that provides for a temporary reduction in the fuel levy amid elevated global oil prices. Data released earlier showed South Africa's inflation rate rose further to 4.5% in September from 4.4% in August, though slightly below the expected 4.7%, mainly due to rising domestic fuel prices. Meanwhile, the statement underscored the government’s commitment to fiscal discipline, with estimates for both the debt-to-GDP ratio and the budget deficit for the current fiscal year (2026/27) revised lower and expected to decline further in the years ahead. The Treasury also released draft fiscal anchor legislation for public comment, expected to be tabled in parliament next year. Nevertheless, the 2026 GDP growth forecast was cut to 1.2% from 1.6%.

【《電動汽車動力蓄電池在役檢測試驗方法》國標釋出】記者今天（21日）瞭解到，市場監管總局近日釋出國家標準《電動汽車動力蓄電池在役檢測試驗方法》，自2027年3月1日正式實施。該標準適用於電動汽車在役動力蓄電池檢測，覆蓋電池隱患排查、二手車交易與功能檢測等應用場景。該標準規定了動力蓄電池在服役狀態下通用測試條件，明確外觀、氣密性、絕緣、熱管理系統等檢測專案，給出可用容量保持率、電壓與內阻一致性的估算方式。該標準的釋出解決了新能源汽車動力蓄電池在役檢測缺乏統一試驗方法的問題，提升電池技術狀況和交易資訊透明度、保障消費者權益，推動新能源汽車後市場規範執行，以標準化檢測、試驗方式賦能新能源汽車產業高質量可持續發展。（央視新聞）

Mexican Retail Sales Beat Expectations in August — Mexico’s retail sales rose 3.3% year-on-year in August 2026, accelerating from 1.8% growth in July and beating expectations for a 2% increase. Sales growth was led by online channels, printed catalogs, TV, and similar platforms (17.6%), followed by healthcare products (9.3%), hardware, paint, and glass products (8.3%), household appliances, computers, home décor, and second-hand goods (6.2%), and textiles, accessories, footwear, and clothing (5.6%). ### 2026-10-21 20:19:45  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Africa #Government Budget #Importance 0

South Africa Sees Fiscal Outlook Improve — In its Medium-Term Budget Policy Statement, South African Finance Minister Enoch Godongwana confirmed public debt peaked at 78.4% of GDP in the 2025/26 fiscal year, slightly below the level projected in February’s budget, and is expected to decline to 78.2% in 2026/27 and 77.9% in 2027/28. The consolidated budget deficit for the current fiscal year (2026/27) is now forecast at 3.9% of GDP, down slightly from the previous estimate of 4%. The improving fiscal outlook is supported by a primary budget surplus expected to rise for a fourth consecutive year, reaching 1.7% of GDP in 2026/27, driven by stronger tax revenues from corporate income tax, VAT and higher commodity prices. To reinforce fiscal discipline, the government released draft legislation establishing a fiscal anchor for public comment, with the bill expected to be tabled in Parliament next year. However, the 2026 economic growth was revised down to 1.2%, from 1.6% projected in February.

US 10-Year Treasury Yield Steady on Wednesday — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note held steady at around 5.1% on Wednesday as investors continued to assess the outlook for the Fed’s monetary policy decision next week. Markets are pricing in an approximately 85% probability that the Fed will leave interest rates unchanged, while the odds of a 25bps rate hike in December stand at around 75%. Oil prices were up on Wednesday following fresh missile strikes targeting tankers in the Strait of Hormuz and Saudi energy facilities. Housing data released this week pointed to mortgage rates weighing on demand, while CPI and PPI reports published last week signaled mounting inflationary pressures. Treasury yields had surged to multi-decade highs earlier this month amid a global bond selloff, driven by persistent energy-related inflation, mounting government debt, and deteriorating fiscal conditions across major economies.

India Infrastructure Output Grows at Fastest Pace in Nearly 3 Years — Infrastructure output in India surged 7.9% from the previous year in September of 2026, the strongest pace of increase since November 2023, following an upwardly revised 5.9% gain in the prior month. Growth was driven by a rebound in the output of coal, rising 10.7% following a 3.8% decline, while production grew significantly further of iron ores (18.4% vs 5.5%) and steel (4.9% vs 3.5%). Stronger output growth of refined products to 10.3% from 5.6% also contributed. However, production of other energy products continued to decline, but at a slower pace, including crude oil (-1.4% vs -3.6%) and natural gas (-4.6% vs -4.9%), and also fertilizers (-11.7% vs -12.4%).

### 2026-10-21 19:19:18  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Africa #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

South Africa Retail Trade Rises More than Expected — Retail sales in South Africa rose by 3.3% from a year ago in August 2026, following a 3.4% advance in July. The reading exceeded market expectations of 3%, keeping retail sales growth close to its strongest level since early 2026. The increase was supported by strong performances across several retail segments, particularly textile, clothing, footwear and leather goods, where sales rose 6.5%. Other retailers recorded the fastest growth among the main contributors, at 8.1%, while general dealers saw a more modest 2.0% increase. Specialised food, beverage and tobacco stores also performed well, with sales up 5.8%. On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, retail sales shrank by 0.3%, after a downwardly revised 2.4% rise in the prior month. Retail trade sales grew by 2.6% in the three months ended August 2026 compared with the three months ended August 2025.

US Mortgage Applications Fall for Seventh Straight Week — US mortgage applications fell 5.3% in the week ending October 16, marking a seventh consecutive weekly decline and reaching their lowest level since January 2025, according to the Mortgage Bankers Association. The continued drop reflects elevated borrowing costs, with the average 30-year fixed mortgage rate edging lower last week but remaining near a three-year high. Rising bond yields and expectations that the Federal Reserve will keep interest rates higher for longer, amid renewed inflationary pressures from surging energy prices linked to the Iran war, have continued to weigh on housing demand. Applications for home purchases declined by 7.3% from the previous week, while refinancing applications fell 2%.

### 2026-10-21 18:45:35  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 2

Crude Oil Rises to 5-Week High — Crude oil climbed to around $100 a barrel on Wednesday, its highest level since September 17 as continued attacks in the Strait of Hormuz heightened concerns over global supply security. The UK Maritime Trade Operations agency reported that a vessel had been struck near Oman, while Iran’s state-run IRIB News said a supertanker had hit a sea mine in the strait. Tehran has intensified attacks in recent weeks, with more incidents reportedly targeting vessels’ engine rooms and raising risks for maritime traffic. However, oil flows through the strategic waterway have remained relatively strong. US Secretary of State Marco Rubio said Tuesday that shipments through the chokepoint were approaching prewar levels, while data from Kpler and Vortexa showed broader Middle Eastern exports had increased since the summer. Investors are also awaiting US inventory figures due later Wednesday, after the American Petroleum Institute reported a 4.9 million-barrel decline in crude stocks last week.

【伊朗外長：美國政府必須真正承認，軍事手段已經走不通】伊朗外長在媒體發文：上個月在聯合國大會期間，我同多位外長舉行會談，各方議程不盡相同，但我方傳遞的資訊始終如一：伊朗希望同美國達成一項公正、體面的協議。想要實現這一目標，白宮必須拿出誠意，展現永久停止侵略的真實意願。 伊朗堅定致力於尋求有尊嚴、和平的衝突解決方案，同時我們已做好充分自衛準備。特朗普發動這場戰爭，源於對伊朗抵抗意志根本性的誤判。種種事實擺在眼前，但他似乎仍不斷收到錯誤資訊，認為伊朗武裝力量沒有保家衛國的能力。 白宮既無意就後續路線達成共識，還持續揚言升級武力，因此很難期待局勢出現進展。即便如此，作為職業外交官，我有責任盡一切努力給外交談判一線生機，哪怕成功的希望渺茫。 出路是存在的，但前提只有一條：美國政府必須真正承認，軍事手段已經走不通。戰事持續近八個月，美方攻擊伊朗時公開提出的目標無一達成，這一點本就應當十分清楚。 如果我們看到確鑿證據，證明美國真心希望達成公正體面的方案、永久結束這場衝突，伊朗願意再給外交一次機會。特朗普開戰之初就誤判了伊朗，現在重複同樣的錯誤，只會招致更加慘痛的結局。

【範波會見奧樂齊中國區董事長陳佳一行】

範波代表蘇州市委、蘇州市政府對奧樂齊投資蘇州表示感謝。他說，當前蘇州正深入學習貫徹習近平總書記對江蘇、蘇州工作重要講話重要指示精神，按照市黨代會部署安排，大力實施消費投資擴容增效工程和服務業擴能提質行動，協同上海等地辦好系列促消費活動，不斷激發消費市場活力，加快建設國際化現代化人民城市。奧樂齊是全球領先的零售企業，近年來加大在蘇州全域佈局力度，體現了對蘇州營商環境和消費市場的高度認可。希望奧樂齊持續擴大在蘇投資，加密門店佈局，提高本地供應鏈配套比重，不斷拓展新業態新模式，深度融入蘇州“一刻鐘幸福生活圈”建設，更好滿足群眾多元化消費需求，讓“生活在這裡很有福氣”更加可感可及。蘇州將深入開展“三服務”專項行動，打造“有呼必應、事事閉環”營商環境品牌，全方位服務企業深耕發展。

陳佳感謝蘇州給予奧樂齊的支援幫助。她表示，蘇州經濟活力十足、市場空間廣闊、消費需求旺盛，是零售企業深耕發展的沃土。奧樂齊堅定看好蘇州發展，將堅持本土化策略、標準化模式，紮根社群拓展門店佈局，創新場景提升業態品質，持續深化與蘇州供應鏈對接合作，不斷提升蘇州區域總部能級，努力把“好品質，夠低價”的體驗帶給更多蘇州消費者，更好服務蘇州零售業創新發展。

奧樂齊中國區董事會成員、首席財務官林曉陽；副市長季晶，蘇州工業園區主要負責同志參加會見。（蘇州日報）

Slovenia Jobless Rate Steady at 4.4% — Slovenia’s unemployment rate remained unchanged at 4.4% in August 2026, matching the previous month. The number of unemployed people decreased by 26 month-on-month and 1,076 year-on-year to 43,231. Those aged 40–49 accounted for the largest share of unemployment at 24%, followed by those aged over 55 at 23.96% and those aged 30–39 at 21.6%. Meanwhile, the share of unemployed people with tertiary education rose to 19.6% from 19.3% in the previous month, while first-time job seekers accounted for 16.4%, down slightly from 16.6%. The share of those unemployed for more than a year increased to 38.4% from 37.9%, while the share of those unemployed for more than two years edged down to 20.7% from 20.9%.

### 2026-10-21 18:16:24  #Trading Economics #Economy #Latvia #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Latvia Producer Inflation Accelerates in September — Producer prices in Latvia rose by 3.1% year-on-year in September 2026, accelerating from an upwardly revised 2.7% increase in the previous month. Price growth in manufacturing remained steady at 2.5%, supported by faster increases in textiles (6.3% vs. 5.4% in August), paper and paper products (4.0% vs. 2.6%), chemicals and chemical products (9.3% vs. 8.2%), and rubber and plastic products (6.5% vs. 6.2%). In contrast, price growth slowed for beverages (2.6% vs. 3.7%), wood and products of wood and cork, except furniture (4.2% vs. 5.4%), and printing and reproduction of recorded media (2.4% vs. 5.0%). Moreover, prices rose faster in electricity, gas, steam, and air-conditioning supply (4.3% vs. 1.6%). Meanwhile, price growth moderated in mining and quarrying (1.9% vs. 4.9%) and water supply, sewerage, waste management, and remediation activities (8.8% vs. 9.1%). On a monthly basis, producer prices rose by 0.5% in September, down from an upwardly revised 0.6% increase in August.

【National Financial Regulatory Administration convenes IAC 2026 meeting in Beijing】China's National Financial Regulatory Administration International Advisory Committee (IAC) met in Beijing on Oct. 19-20. Director Ding Xiangqun reviewed recent progress and set regulatory priorities for the 15th Five-Year period: risk prevention, stronger supervision, promoting high-quality development and expanded high-level opening-up. Delegates discussed financial risks under the current international environment and strategies for resolving financial institution stress, and reaffirmed confidence in China's economic resilience after achievements in the 14th Five-Year period. The meeting urged stronger policy coordination and cross-border regulatory cooperation amid rising global uncertainty; enhanced cross-border risk prevention and response; guidance for financial firms to adopt AI while tightening controls on new tech-related financial risks; and continuous improvement of early-warning and resolution mechanisms to prevent systemic risk.

### 2026-10-21 18:13:12  #宏觀 #公司 #市場 #國際

【國家金融監督管理總局召開國際諮詢委員會2026年會議】國家金融監督管理總局國際諮詢委員會（IAC）2026年會議於10月19日—20日在北京召開。中共中央政治局委員、國務院副總理何立峰19日晚在釣魚臺國賓館會見金融監管總局國際諮詢委員會委員時表示，中國正全面落實“十五五”規劃綱要，將繼續堅定不移擴大金融領域高水平對外開放，加快建設金融強國。歡迎更多外資金融機構來華投資興業，深化對華合作，為中國式現代化建設提供高質量金融服務。金融監管總局黨委書記、局長丁向群出席會議，介紹了中國銀行業保險業最新進展，以及“十五五”時期金融監管總局在防風險、強監管、促高質量發展、擴大高水平對外開放等方面的工作重點。會議圍繞當前國際形勢下的金融風險和監管、金融機構風險處置策略等議題進行了深入討論。與會代表充分肯定了中國在“十四五”時期取得的發展成就，表示對“十五五”時期中國經濟的韌性與長期發展前景充滿信心。會議認為，在當前國際環境不確定性上升的背景下，各國監管當局應加強政策協調和跨境監管合作，做好跨境風險防範和應對；引導金融機構在利用人工智慧等新技術提升效率的同時，加強新型金融風險防範；不斷健全金融風險預警處置機制，守住不發生系統性風險底線。美國聯邦存款保險公司前主席希拉·貝爾，香港證券及期貨事務監察委員會前主席沈聯濤，香港保險業監管局主席姚建華，安聯集團（義大利）董事長、歐洲保險業聯合會前主席塞爾吉奧·巴比諾特，友邦保險集團獨立非執行主席兼獨立非執行董事杜嘉祺，瑞穗金融集團董事長武英克，渣打集團主席羅睿思，澳門金融管理局行政管理委員會主席黃善文等委員出席了會議。金融監管總局黨委委員、副局長肖遠企、叢林，駐總局紀檢監察組組長、黨委委員楊國瑞，首席風險官、首席律師，金融監管總局相關司局和地方局負責同志，外交部、財政部、人民銀行、證監會、北京市委金融辦、西城區政府相關代表參加了會議。此次會議在2026金融街論壇年會期間舉行，國際諮詢委員會委員出席論壇年會開幕式、閉幕式和相關會議活動。

【李強同俄羅斯總理米舒斯京共同主持中俄總理第三十一次定期會晤】10月21日上午，國務院總理李強在聖彼得堡同俄羅斯總理米舒斯京共同主持中俄總理第三十一次定期會晤。李強表示，近些年，面對複雜動盪的國際形勢，中俄雙方在兩國元首戰略引領下，攜手並肩、團結向前，以富有活力、韌性的合作對沖外部環境的不穩定不確定性，合作道路越走越寬，步伐越來越穩。當前，中俄新時代全面戰略協作夥伴關係正在進入更有作為、更快發展的新階段。中方願同俄方發揚相互尊重、互利共贏精神，推動各領域合作不斷走深走實，把兩國元首重要共識更好轉化為實際行動和具體成效，更好服務兩國發展振興，更多惠及兩國人民。米舒斯京表示，今年是《俄中睦鄰友好合作條約》簽署25週年。當前，俄中新時代全面戰略協作夥伴關係處於歷史最高水平，樹立了國家間關係的典範。面對國際形勢不確定性，兩國經貿合作勢頭良好。俄方願同中方在互利共贏、相互尊重、睦鄰友好基礎上，進一步加強各領域務實合作，維護兩國共同利益。李強和米舒斯京聽取了雙方代表彙報中俄投資合作委員會、中俄能源合作委員會、中俄人文合作委員會，中俄總理定期會晤委員會，中國東北地區和俄羅斯遠東地區政府間合作委員會工作。兩國總理充分肯定各委員會近一年來的工作。雙方一致認為，元首定向領航是中俄關系穩健發展的重要保障，要以兩國元首重要共識為根本遵循，推進經貿投資、能源資源、互聯互通、航空航天、金融、工業等重點領域合作，培育人工智慧、生物技術、數字經濟等前沿領域發展新動能，共同辦好“中俄教育年”，加強旅遊、文化、青年、媒體等人文交流，激發地方合作活力，密切多邊事務溝通協作，推動取得更多務實成果。會晤後，兩國總理簽署《中俄總理第三十一次定期會晤聯合公報》。雙方還達成經貿、航天、科技、教育等領域合作檔案。（新華社）

【China central offices urge SOEs to set up independently accounted, performance-exempt R&D reserve funds with error-tolerance】The CPC Central Committee General Office and State Council General Office issued opinions to deepen integration of technology and industrial innovation. They call for improving mechanisms for enterprises to routinely participate in national technology and industrial strategy, planning and policy-making. The National Natural Science Foundation pilot will be opened to private firms; the scope and size of corporate joint innovation funds will be expanded. The document encourages state-owned enterprises to accelerate establishment of independently accounted R&D reserve funds that are exempt from value-preservation/appreciation performance assessments and that include error-tolerance and correction provisions. It urges technology SMEs to boost R&D spending and raises the preferential R&D super-deduction rate. The guidance backs enterprise-led construction of innovation platforms, opens major national science infrastructure, large research instruments and national scientific data centers to firms, and steps up corporate support under national talent programs while strengthening enterprise postdoctoral stations.

【General Office of the CPC Central Committee and General Office of the State Council call for high-quality 'technology board' in bond market】The General Office of the CPC Central Committee and the General Office of the State Council issued opinions urging deeper integration of tech innovation, industrial innovation and finance and promoting a tech–industry–finance virtuous cycle. They call for a tech-finance system aligned with tech firms’ full life-cycle financing needs and for use of the national venture capital guiding fund. Government-backed venture funds and state-capital VC are to prioritise early-stage, smaller-ticket, long-duration and hard‑tech investments and to strengthen long-cycle performance assessment, differentiated supervision and exit mechanisms. The paper promotes an "innovation points system" to quantify tech SME innovation capacity, mandates high-quality construction of a bond-market "technology board", and seeks to establish a technology insurance policy framework.

Euro Weakens Amid French Fiscal Concerns — The euro remained under pressure around $1.122, as rising French borrowing costs and worsening tensions between the US and Europe over Russia’s war in Ukraine weighed on sentiment. France’s 10-year OAT yield climbed above 5%, its highest level since 2002, reflecting mounting concerns over France’s public finances and social unrest. Meanwhile, relations between Washington and European allies have deteriorated as US President Donald Trump deepens his engagement with Russian President Vladimir Putin. Recent moves, including easing sanctions on Moscow and questioning Ukraine’s political leadership, have heightened uncertainty over Western unity and future support for Kyiv. Elsewhere, rising oil prices amid the ongoing Iran war have intensified inflation concerns and increased expectations of tighter monetary policy, weighing on Europe’s growth outlook. Political uncertainty ahead of key elections across major European economies has added to the pressure on the single currency.

【Franklin Templeton warns markets unprepared for potential eurozone debt crisis 2.0】Franklin Templeton’s fixed-income team, led by global CIO Sonal Desai, warned sustained selling of French government bonds could escalate into a eurozone debt crisis potentially more severe than the crisis of the early 2010s and said markets are unprepared. On Tuesday the 10-year French yield premium to German Bunds topped 150bps for the first time since January 2012. Desai said France — the eurozone’s second-largest economy (GDP ~€3.0t) with roughly €3.6t of debt — would present a far larger stress test for the EU than past sovereign cases; its debt stock is more than ten times Greece’s, making simple bailout options infeasible.

### 2026-10-21 17:55:56  #MKT News #Star Stocks #NVIDIA #Market Themes #AI Revolution #Magnificent 7 #Market Regions #Europe & UK #NVDA.O #Impact bullish

【NVIDIA launches DSX POD AI factory to accelerate European infrastructure deployment】NVIDIA launched DSX POD, a factory-integrated AI factory reference design based on the Vera Rubin architecture, targeting faster European AI infrastructure rollouts. Partners build modular container units that are fully integrated and validated in manufacturing; once on site they require only connection to pre-provisioned power, cooling and networking to operate, NVIDIA said, cutting deployment time from about six months to as little as 14 days. The design moves compute, networking, cabling, rack-level power and liquid cooling installation into the factory, reducing on-site planning, construction, skilled labor and logistics and lowering risks from component delays and error-prone field work. NVIDIA said factory-tested containers shorten the path to model training and production inference, enabling customers to start generating tokens and revenue earlier. JENSEN HUANG: AI factory creates value when it starts producing tokens, not when construction finishes.

German Bunds Remain Supported by Safe-Haven Demand — Germany’s 10-year Bund yield held just above 3.4%, supported by safe-haven demand, while French and other highly indebted euro-area bonds remained under pressure from fiscal concerns and political uncertainty. The spread between French OATs and German Bunds widened beyond 155 basis points, its highest level since the eurozone sovereign debt crisis. Global bond yields have risen this year amid surging energy prices and expectations of tighter monetary policy. France has come under particular pressure, with its 10-year bond yield reaching a fresh 24-year high amid mounting fiscal concerns and social unrest. Anti-government protests and budget negotiations in a deeply divided Parliament have raised doubts about the government’s ability to implement deficit-reduction measures ahead of the 2027 presidential election.

French 10-Year Yield Tops 5% — France’s 10-year OAT yield surged above 5%, reaching a new 24-year high as investors grew increasingly concerned about the country’s fragile public finances and mounting social unrest. The spread over 10-year German Bunds widened to around 155 basis points, its highest level since the eurozone sovereign debt crisis. Global bond yields have climbed this year amid surging energy prices and expectations of further monetary tightening, while highly indebted euro-area countries, including France, face additional pressure from rising borrowing costs and growing debt burdens. Meanwhile, the country's minority government faces a difficult task in passing its budget and reducing the deficit, amid parliamentary divisions, widespread protests and uncertainty ahead of the 2027 presidential election. Investors are also awaiting Moody’s long-anticipated credit rating review on Friday, with the risk of a downgrade adding to pressure on French bonds.

【迪安診斷：前三季度扣非淨利增超10倍，醫療AI三年規劃目標年增速100%  】10月21日，迪安診斷（300244.SZ）舉辦投資者關係活動，通報了前三季度經營情況及AI戰略全景。經營層面，公司前三季度實現營業收入74.09億元，同比下降2.07%；歸母淨利潤4.82億元，同比增長750.28%；扣非淨利潤4.18億元，同比增長1074.80%；經營性現金流8.24億元，保持在歷年高位。單季度看，第三季度營收26.35億元，同比增長0.23%，歸母淨利潤2.50億元，同比增長438.03%，營收逐季改善趨勢明確。分業務看，診斷服務業務收入26.45億元，同比增長0.87%，呈逐季改善態勢，累計增速由負轉正；渠道產品業務收入47.10億元，同比下降4.47%，降幅收窄；自有產品業務收入3.00億元，同比增長5.70%，延續增長。AI戰略方面，2025年6月公司首次釋出AI三年戰略規劃，明確“深耕醫療健康場景，以‘AI+資料’為引擎，引領產業生態智慧化未來”的AI戰略定位，並明確未來3年集團整體AI業務的業績貢獻每年實現100%的增長。公司依託全國網路積累了22PB多組學、多模態資料，每年約新增1.6PB資料量；目前已清洗治理完成6PB+高質量資料。公司擁有兩款核心自研大模型：“啟迪索微”多模態大模型（已於2025年透過國家網信辦演算法備案）和專注病理領域的“靈眸”多模態大模型，並推出面向不同客戶群體的“3+1”產品矩陣。

### 2026-10-21 17:29:40  #MKT News #09868.HK #Impact mixed

【He Xiaopeng says L3 safety is questionable】XPeng (09868.HK) chairman He Xiaopeng told the World Intelligent Connected Vehicles Conference that the industry should skip L3 because it shifts liability to the system without improving user experience or safety. He said L5 must be redefined—cars are passive automated platforms while robots perform active 24/7 tasks—and that prior L5 definitions focused on technology rather than safety and scene controllability. He added regulators must prioritise safety and governance for higher-level autonomy to progress, and that the commercially relevant opportunities are L2, L4 and a redefined L5, with L3 essentially an enhanced form of L2.

### 2026-10-21 17:28:56  #其他

【何小鵬：L3是不是更安全需要打個問號】小鵬汽車董事長何小鵬今日在世界智慧網聯汽車大會上表示，自動駕駛應該跳過L3，L3模式下安全責任轉移給系統，但它只是轉移了責任，使用者體驗沒有最佳化，重點是安全性沒有變好。未來L5會重新被定義，汽車自動駕駛屬於被動式自動駕駛；而機器人是需要24小時主動執行任務的；過去L5是純從技術維度定義的，沒有從安全、場景可控角度去定義。他認為，各個國家監管體系想要管控自動駕駛、支撐技術向更高等級演進，一定要安全、可管控。基於這個邏輯判斷未來自動駕駛賽道真正有發展機會的是L2、L4和重新定義後的L5，而L3本質上只是L2的一種增強形態。（第一財經）

### 2026-10-21 17:15:49  #Trading Economics #Economy #Iceland #Unemployment Rate #Importance 1

Iceland Jobless Rate Falls to 3-Month Low — Iceland’s seasonally adjusted unemployment rate fell to 5.7% in September 2026 from a six-month high of 6.8% in the previous month. This marked the lowest reading since June, as the number of unemployed persons decreased by 2.6 thousand month-on-month to 13.3 thousand. By gender, the unemployment rate for men dropped sharply by 6.1% to 7.8 thousand, while that of women increased by 5.1% to 5.5 thousand. Moreover, the youth unemployment rate, which measures job-seekers between 16–24 years old, climbed to 16.1% from a revised 14.4% in August. Meanwhile, the employment rate rose by 75.0% from 73.8%, with the number of employed individuals rising by 2.1 thousand to 220.7 thousand. The labor force participation rate ticked up to 79.7% from 79.2%. On a non-seasonally adjusted basis, the unemployment rate fell by 5.1% in September from 6.0% in the preceding period.

Belgium Consumer Pessimism Highest in 18 Months — Belgium's consumer confidence indicator fell to -13 in October 2026, marking its eighth consecutive month in negative territory and the lowest reading since April 2025, down from -10 in September. Households' assessments of their current financial situation edged down to 18 from 19, while pessimism about major purchases deepened, with the indicator falling to -38 from -31. Saving intentions also weakened to -34 from -31. Over the next 12 months, households expect prices to continue rising, while intentions to make major purchases declined to -21 from -19. Expectations for personal financial conditions deteriorated to -7 from -2, while sentiment regarding Belgium's economic outlook worsened to -44 from -41. Meanwhile, households see unemployment rising.

Croatia Jobless Rate Steady at 3.4% — Croatia’s registered unemployment rate remained unchanged at 3.4% for the third consecutive month. The number of unemployed persons declined 1% month-on-month to 62.6 thousand, while total employment edged down 0.6% to 1.767 million. The labor force also decreased 0.6% to 1.829 million. Meanwhile, paid employment in legal entities fell 0.1% to 1.518 million, while employment in crafts, trades, and freelances dropped 3.5% to 231.8 thousand, and employment among private farmers edged down 0.2% to 17.2 thousand. Compared with September 2025, the labor market remained weaker overall, with the labor force down 1.4% and total employment declining 0.8%, while paid employment in legal entities fell 1.1%. Over the same period, the number of registered unemployed persons dropped 16%.

### 2026-10-21 17:10:26  #公司 #市場 #觀點

【聯想據悉尋求20億美元貸款 以更低成本為債務再融資】知情人士稱，聯想集團正在尋求一筆20億美元貸款，其定價將低於公司計劃再融資的一筆現有貸款。在交易稀缺之際，亞洲銀行仍熱衷於向中國大型企業放貸。因涉及非公開資訊而不願具名的知情人士稱，這筆五年期貸款已啟動銀團組建，定價為擔保隔夜融資利率(SOFR)加75個基點。這一利差明顯低於聯想2022年獲得的同等規模貸款，當時的定價為SOFR加110個基點，另加信用調整利差。知情人士表示，這一調整與基準利率從倫敦銀行間同業拆借利率(Libor)轉向SOFR有關。聯想此次籌措新貸款之際，其人工智慧基礎設施業務持續增長。截至6月的季度，公司營收躍升43%，並上調了財年業績展望。與此同時，亞洲整體貸款市場正面臨交易荒，尤其是中國借款人由於可以在境內獲得成本更低的資金，對離岸貸款的需求有限。法國巴黎銀行、花旗集團、星展銀行、滙豐控股和摩根大通香港分行為聯想這筆貸款的協調行。

### 2026-10-21 16:50:10  #Trading Economics #Economy #Slovenia #Producer Prices Change #Importance 0

Slovenia Producer Inflation at Highest Since July 2023 — Slovenia’s producer prices rose 3.9% year-on-year in September 2026, the most since July 2023, compared to 3.5% in the previous month. Prices increased for electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning supply (7.4% vs 1.9% in August), mining and quarrying (9.3% vs 8.4%), and manufacturing (3.8% vs 3.7%), with basic metals posting the steepest increase (14.3%), followed by other manufacturing (14.1%) and leather and leather products (10.2%). Meanwhile, deflation in water supply remained unchanged at 4.0%. Among the main industrial groups, prices rose for energy (6.2% vs 2.6%) and capital goods (3.3% vs 2.4%), while costs eased for intermediate goods (5.7% vs 5.8%) and were unchanged for consumer goods (1.0%). On a monthly basis, producer prices rose 0.1% in September, easing from 0.3% in August.

### 2026-10-21 16:49:15  #公司 #焦點 #國際

【範波、王維會見三星電子副社長李坽洙一行】10月20日，三星電子蘇州研發中心啟動儀式在蘇州工業園區舉行。蘇州市委書記範波會見了三星電子副社長、生產技術研究所所長李坽洙一行，並出席啟動儀式。市委副書記、市長王維參加會見。

三星電子蘇州研發中心是三星海外首家智慧製造研究所，將依託中國完備的產業鏈與人才優勢，主攻製造自動化、機器人、智慧生產系統的研發創新，支撐三星全球業務迭代升級。

範波、王維代表市委、市政府對三星電子蘇州研發中心啟動表示祝賀。範波說，蘇州是中國東部地區重要的中心城市，當前正牢牢把握上海與蘇州重點領域同城化發展、上海（長三角）國際科技創新中心關鍵支點、國家新型工業化示範區三大戰略性抓手，因地制宜發展新質生產力，著力建設“1030”產業體系，培育壯大“10+10”重點產業，打造具有國際競爭力的“智慧製造之城”。希望三星集團持續擴大在蘇產業投資和專案佈局，深化產業鏈協同，放大研發中心效應，聯合開展技術攻關、市場拓展、人才引育，實現更高水平互利共贏。蘇州將不斷擴大高水平對外開放，深入開展“三服務”專項行動，著力打造“有呼必應、事事閉環”營商環境品牌，服務企業創新發展。

李坽洙感謝蘇州給予企業的支援幫助。他說，蘇州產業基礎深厚、營商環境良好、發展前景廣闊。將以研發中心啟動為契機，匯入更多創新資源、高階人才，深化與蘇州產業鏈創新鏈對接合作，推動形成更多標誌性合作專案，共享中國市場廣闊機遇。（蘇州日報）

### 2026-10-21 16:26:01  #Trading Economics #Economy #Greece #Current Account #Importance 1

Greece Current Account Surplus Widens to Highest Since 2015 — Greece’s current account surplus widened to EUR 1.743 billion in August 2026 from EUR 1.210 billion in the same month last year, marking the largest surplus since August 2015. The services account surplus increased to EUR 4.552 billion from EUR 4.498 billion, driven largely by higher revenues in travel, transport, and other services. Moreover, the primary income deficit narrowed sharply to EUR 0.021 billion from EUR 0.773 billion a year earlier. On the other hand, the goods account shortfall expanded to EUR 2.342 billion from EUR 2.199 billion, as imports (EUR 6.507 billion) outpaced exports (EUR 4.164 billion). The secondary income deficit also rose to EUR 0.447 billion from EUR 0.316 billion.

National Bank of Georgia Holds Key Rate Steady — The National Bank of Georgia held its key policy rate steady at 8.25% at its October 2026 meeting, maintaining a moderately tight stance amid persistent inflationary pressures. Annual inflation rose to 5.6% in September, well above the bank’s 3% target, with energy prices contributing around 2 percentage points. The central bank raised its 2026 inflation forecast to 5.4% from 5.2%, while expecting inflation to average 3.5% in 2027 before gradually converging toward target from the second half of the year. Economic activity remained stronger than expected, with growth averaging 7.6% in the first eight months of 2026, prompting the bank to raise its 2026 GDP growth forecast to 7.2% from 6.5%. The bank warned that further escalation in the Middle East could push energy prices and inflation higher, potentially prompting additional rate hikes, while easing tensions and lower energy prices could allow faster monetary policy normalization.

Lebanon Inflation Rate at 4-Month High — The annual inflation rate in Lebanon rose to 17.5% in September 2026 from 16.7% in the previous month, marking its highest reading since May 2026. Prices increased at a faster pace for several categories, including housing and utilities (20.4% vs. 17.9% in August), transportation (35.2% vs. 31.7%), clothing and footwear (3.1% vs. 1.2%), furnishings, household equipment and routine household maintenance (9.7% vs. 9.6%), and restaurants and hotels (15.5% vs. 14.3%). Meanwhile, deflation eased for communication (-0.7% vs. -0.8%), while inflation remained unchanged for alcoholic beverages and tobacco (7.1%), education (35.7%), and miscellaneous goods and services (15.7%). In contrast, price growth slowed for food and non-alcoholic beverages (10.0% vs. 11.7%), health (7.3% vs. 7.4%), and recreation and culture (43.6% vs. 45.7%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 1.4%, unchanged from the previous month.

South Africa Inflation Rises Less Than Forecast — The annual inflation rate rose for the second month to 4.5% in September 2026 from 4.4% in August, but below the expected 4.7%. Still, the headline inflation remained above the SARB’s 3% target and the upper bound of its 2%–4% tolerance band. The acceleration was largely driven by rising transport prices, which climbed further by 10.8%, compared to 8.8% in August, largely reflecting a sharper increase in fuel prices (27.9% vs 20%). Diesel prices jumped 46.0%, while petrol prices rose 25.0%, following domestic price hikes. Food inflation also rose slightly faster to 1.2% from 1.1% in August. The core inflation, which excludes prices of food, non-alcoholic beverages, fuel and energy, edged down to 4.0% in September from 4.1% in August. On a monthly basis, the CPI went up by 0.4%, after being flat in the previous month.

UK Natural Gas Prices Remain Elevated — UK natural gas prices rose above 217 pence per therm, hovering near their highest level in almost four years amid tight supply conditions. Gas exports from the Middle East, which accounted for around a fifth of global LNG supply before the war began, remained largely disrupted, with flows approximately 80% below year-earlier levels. Britain’s limited gas-storage capacity could be depleted during an exceptionally cold winter, as unusually low European gas reserves increase reliance on imported LNG. Government data show that, as of early October, the UK had storage capacity of about 3.2 billion cubic metres, leaving the country more exposed to price shocks. Meanwhile, the approval of the Jackdaw gas field could provide some relief, which is expected to start supplying gas to the UK by the end of this month and increase output by around 6% at its peak.

BI also kept the rate on its lending facility at 6.5% but raised the overnight deposit facility rate (DFR) by 25 bps to 5%. BI lowered banks’ reserve requirement ratio (RRR) to 7% from 9% for conventional banks and to 5.5% from 7.5% for Islamic banks. Japanese Stocks Advance on AI Optimism — Japan’s Nikkei 225 Index climbed 0.85% to close at 64,621 on Wednesday, recovering from one-month lows as AI-related stocks led the gains. Strong results from server manufacturer Super Micro Computer improved expectations for AI spending, while robust earnings from SpaceX, supported by growth in its AI and satellite internet businesses, further lifted sentiment. Among the top tech performers in Japan were Kioxia Holdings (2.8%), Advantest (1.7%), SoftBank Group (1.5%), Lasertec (1.5%), and Taiyo Yuden (1.7%). Financial stocks also rebounded, including Mitsubishi UFJ (2.4%) and Sumitomo Mitsui (2.3%). However, the market retreated from its session highs as oil prices climbed again amid renewed uncertainty surrounding US-Iran negotiations, heightening inflation concerns. Meanwhile, data showed that Japanese exports grew less than expected in September but extended their expansion for a 13th consecutive month, supported by strong demand for semiconductors and AI-related products.

### 2026-10-21 14:52:45  #Trading Economics #Economy #Indonesia #Loan Growth #Importance 1

Indonesia Loan Growth Eases to 3-Month Low — Indonesia’s loan growth rose 13.26% year-on-year in September 2026, following a 13.36% increase in August, marking the slowest growth since June. By usage, investment loans led the expansion, growing 23.2%, followed by working capital loans (11.89%) and consumer loans (4.86%). Meanwhile, undisbursed loan facilities stood at IDR 2,562.57 trillion, equivalent to 21.70% of total credit limits. Lending prospects are also supported by steady third-party fund growth of 8.22% year-on-year. The banking industry's liquid assets-to-deposits (AL/DPK) ratio stood at 24.08% in September, while the non-performing loan ratio stood at 2.11% (gross) and 0.84% (net). The central bank expects loan growth to reach 8–12% for the full year, reflecting its accommodative macroprudential stance to support the economy.

European Stocks Poised to Open Higher — European equity markets were set to open in positive territory on Wednesday as robust earnings from major US technology companies eased concerns surrounding AI-related investments and lifted global investor sentiment. ### 2026-10-21 14:12:38  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Stock Market #Importance 1

ASX 200 Inches Higher at Finish — Australia's ASX 200 added 0.2% to close at 8,831 on Wednesday, reversing a modest drop in the prior session as U.S. equity futures firmed despite persistent concerns over AI spending. Transport, commercial services, and communication stocks drove the gains, offsetting weakness in logistics, industrial services, and consumer sectors. Four major banks rose between 0.2% and 1.4%, while tech names also gained, led by Xero (3.8%) and Wisetech Global (2.4%). In contrast, Nextdc plunged 40.6%, its steepest drop in nearly nine years, after sharply lowering its FY2027 guidance following delays in securing power network connections. Woodside Energy slipped 0.3% after trimming its 2026 production outlook despite reporting stronger-than-expected Q3 revenue. Mining giants BHP Group and Rio Tinto fell 1.7% and 1.8%, respectively. Traders now await Australia’s flash October PMI data due Friday and comments from Reserve Bank Governor Michele Bullock scheduled to appear before lawmakers on that day.

### 2026-10-21 14:12:27  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Government Budget Value #Importance 1

UK Public Sector Borrowing Slightly Higher than Expected — UK public sector net borrowing (excluding public sector banks) totaled £24.5 billion in September 2026, slightly above £22.8 billion in the same month a year ago and market expectations of £23.9 billion. This was the second-highest September figure on record, behind only that of 2020 (not adjusted for inflation), as spending growth outpaced the rise in receipts. Total spending increased by £6.7 billion year-on-year to £123.8 billion, with central government expenditure rising by £5.9 billion to £111.3 billion, driven by spending on public services, interest, benefits and other costs. Total receipts rose by £4.9 billion to £99.3 billion, with central government revenues up by £4.5 billion to £90.1 billion on higher tax receipts. Borrowing in the financial year to September was £97.8 billion, down £4.4 billion, or 4.3%, from the previous year but £6.0 billion above the OBR’s forecast. Meanwhile, public sector net debt exceeded £3 trillion for the first time, equivalent to 94% of GDP.

### 2026-10-21 14:07:14  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

UK Producer Inflation Hits Near 3-1/2-Year High — Factory gate prices for UK-manufactured goods increased 4.6% year-on-year in September 2026, accelerating from an upwardly revised 3.8% rise in August and surpassing expectations of 4.3% to mark the fastest increase since April 2023. Eight of the 10 product groups made upward contributions to the annual rate, led by a jump in prices of coke and refined petroleum products (63.3% vs. 50.8% in August) amid higher energy costs. Prices for metals, fabricated metal products, and machinery rose 6.1%, unchanged from the revised figure in August. Prices of other manufactured products also increased 3.6%, matching the pace in the previous month. Meanwhile, motor vehicles and other transport equipment made the largest downward contribution, with prices falling 1.2%, followed by food products, where prices dropped 0.6%. On a monthly basis, factory gate prices climbed 0.8%, following a 0.7% rise in August and exceeding forecasts of 0.7%, marking the fastest pace in five months.

---

### 2026-10-21 14:06:48  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Core Inflation Rate #Importance 1

UK Core Inflation Hits 6-Month High, Tops Forecasts — The UK's annual core inflation rate rose to 2.8% in September 2026 from 2.6% in each of the previous four months, marking its highest level since March and slightly exceeding market expectations of 2.7%. The acceleration reflected faster price growth in both goods (3.2% vs 2.7% in August) and services (3.7% vs 3.4%). On a monthly basis, core consumer prices increased by 0.2%, easing from a 0.3% rise in August.

---

### 2026-10-21 14:05:35  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Inflation Rate #Importance 2

UK Inflation Rate Hits 11-Month High — The UK’s annual inflation rate rose to 3.5% in September 2026, its highest since October 2025, from 3.1% in August and above expectations of 3.4%. Transport made the largest upward contribution, with inflation rising to 6.6% from 4.6%, driven mainly by motor fuels. Petrol prices rose 8.2 pence per litre month-on-month to 169.5 pence, the highest since August 2022, while diesel prices increased 10.7 pence to 192.5 pence, the highest since July 2022. Motor fuel inflation accelerated to 29.8% from 23.0%. Inflation also rose for housing and household services (5.3% vs 4.9%), communication (5.8% vs 5.3%) and recreation and culture (2.3% vs 1.6%). Food inflation was unchanged at 1.3%, while inflation for restaurants and hotels eased to 3.8% from 4.1%. Core CPI inflation increased to 2.8% from 2.6%, with goods inflation rising to 3.2% from 2.7% and services inflation to 3.7% from 3.4%. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.4%, following a 0.5% increase in August.

【阿迪達斯2026年第三季度營收同比增長7%，上調全年業績指引】10月21日，阿迪達斯全球公佈了2026年第三季度初步業績：全球營收達72.52億歐元，在貨幣中性下（下同），同比增長7%；營業利潤達10.08億歐元；毛利率提升2.5個百分點至54.3%。第三季度，阿迪達斯全球收到了美國關稅的大部分退款，並據此確認了約2億歐元的退稅，這部分退稅對毛利率的改善做出了貢獻。阿迪達斯全球CEO古爾登表示，“對阿迪達斯而言，這是一個非常出色的季度。我們實現了有史以來最高的單季營收——72.52億歐元，以及有史以來最高的單季營業利潤——10.08億歐元。我們的品牌、產品和人才，是我們最大的力量所在。”介面新聞瞭解到，基於第三季度超預期的表現，阿迪達斯全球上調了2026年全年業績指引，公司預計將實現9%-10%的營收增長，營業利潤預計將提升至25億歐元（此前預計為23億歐元）。

### 2026-10-21 13:24:01  #國際

【特朗普考慮改建白宮新聞簡報室 擬恢復為羅斯福時期室內游泳池】據紐約時報報道，8 月下旬，卡洛琳・萊維特在橢圓形辦公室和特朗普的最後幾場會談中，特朗普當著多名高階幕僚向她提出一項構想：把記者遷出詹姆斯・S・布雷迪新聞簡報室，實質上就是把新聞機構移出白宮。他告訴萊維特，這件事會成為她的 “最後一項遺產”。三位知情、但未獲公開發言授權的人士表示，在萊維特任職期間，該方案始終沒有推進。過去數月，總統已經多次向她提出這項要求。萊維特已於 8 月 27 日正式卸任白宮新聞秘書。特朗普向幕僚表示，他希望恢復羅斯福 1933 年修建的室內泳池，當年修建泳池是為了給罹患小兒麻痺症、腿部行動不便的羅斯福做康復；後來尼克松把泳池加蓋封閉，騰出空間給媒體使用。持有白宮採訪資質的記者，就在簡報室相連的一間小型辦公室內辦公。相較於特朗普長期對新聞記者的抨擊，將媒體遷出白宮這件事本身議題規模不算很大，但具備強烈象徵意義。媒體緊鄰美國行政權力核心，長期以來被視作憲政民主體制中新聞自由重要性的體現。

NZX 50 Ends 1% Higher Ahead of Inflation Data — The NZX 50 advanced 130 points, or 1.0%, to close at 13,761 on Wednesday, erasing losses from the previous three sessions and moving away from its lowest level since September 16, touched a day earlier, ahead of the release of inflation data. Q3 inflation data will be released on Thursday, with the market expecting inflation to ease on both a yearly and quarterly basis. Easing Treasury yields and moderating oil prices also supported sentiment, easing inflation concerns and fears of interest rate hikes after the RBNZ raised interest rates for the second straight time in July and September. Materials, financials, communication services, healthcare, and industrial stocks mainly drove the index, with notable gains from Ebos Group (5.5%), Vulcan Steel (5.2%), Mainfreight (3.2%), Fletcher Building (3.1%), Westpac Banking Corp. 【核能“充電寶”來了！微型反應堆產業平臺成立 我國微堆正式邁入商業化階段】微型核反應堆是一種電功率不超過10兆瓦的先進核能裝置。與傳統核電站不同，它並非簡單地將大型反應堆縮小，而是一種從設計之初就追求固有安全、極致小巧的全新核能裝置。10月20日，四川能源核產業有限公司正式揭牌，核心業務就是聚焦先進微型核反應堆的技術研發與產業化，標誌著我國微堆技術正式從實驗室走向工程化應用的新階段。中國核動力研究設計院副總工程師崔懷明介紹，微堆堆芯大小與家用冰箱相近，兩三個集裝箱就能裝入整個發電系統，無論是地上的卡車、天上的飛機，還是海里的輪船，都能運輸。記者瞭解到，中國核動力研究設計院在全球首次提出了鈾氫鋯燃料鉛鉍冷卻微堆技術路線，它是一種使用鈾氫鋯核燃料並採用液態鉛鉍合金作為冷卻劑的微型核反應堆。（央視）

Thailand Stocks Ease to 3-Week Low — Thailand’s SET50 Index extended losses for a third session by noon Wednesday, slipping toward 1,038 and touching its lowest level since early October. Sentiment weakened after September consumer confidence fell from a six-month high, while headline inflation hit a five-month peak of 2.82%. Still, gains in U.S. stock futures helped limit the downside as markets assessed the latest corporate earnings releases. Locally, Bangkok last week approved a THB 50 billion housing loan program aimed at helping low- and middle-income households access homeownership. The scheme is also designed to support economic activity, with authorities estimating it could generate up to THB 125 billion in property-related output. Sector-wise, industrial services, energy, and transport stocks led the decline, outweighing gains in communications, consumer durables, and financials. Among the laggards, Delta Electronics fell 4.1%, followed by Kiatnakin Phatra Bank (-2.6%), Banpu (-1.4%), and Bangkok Bank (-1.1%).

### 2026-10-21 12:47:28  #宏觀 #國際

【中方回應COP31電氣化倡議：應充分考慮各國國情差異尋求最大公約數】10月21日上午，國務院新聞辦公室舉行新聞釋出會，生態環境部副部長李高介紹中國積極應對氣候變化、參與引領全球氣候治理有關情況，並答記者問。

《聯合國氣候變化框架公約》第三十一次締約方大會（COP31）將於11月9日至20日在土耳其安塔利亞舉行，李高將再次擔任中國代表團團長。中方支援COP31聚焦“落實”，推動多邊程序為各方特別是發展中國家落實國家自主貢獻提供支援、創造條件。

有外媒提問，中方是否支援締約方大會主席關於在安塔利亞確立到2035年實現35%電氣化目標的提議？中方是否支援一個專門聚焦綠色電力的版本？

李高表示，COP31的主席國土耳其發起了電氣化倡議，提出到2035年全球電氣化率35%的目標，由參與的國家自主決定相關的政策和行動。中方認為，提高電氣化水平有助於促進能源綠色低碳轉型和應對氣候變化，中國也正在積極推進新型能源體系和新型電力系統建設，提高終端用能電氣化的水平也是我們的政策方向。

“我們讚賞主席國為推動多邊程序所做的積極努力。我們注意到，倡議得到了不少國家的支援，同時也存在一些不同的意見。有的認為應該聚焦落實已經確立的目標，有的認為要強化綠色電氣化的目標，還有的認為，作為一個自願倡議，不需要搞這種進展的評估等。我們建議主席國進一步充分聽取各方面的意見，充分考慮各國的國情差異，尋求最大公約數，爭取最廣泛支援。”李高說。

It endorses voluntary, non‑binding cooperation under APEC finance and fiscal mechanisms to advance digital infrastructure financing. The statement urges the private sector to deploy catalytic financing tools — including financing leases and asset securitisation — to unlock investment and lower financing barriers, and to scale up capital mobilisation by integrating pension funds, insurers and sovereign wealth funds.

【中方呼籲加快中非共和國政治程序 】中國常駐聯合國副代表孫磊20日在安理會中非共和國問題公開會上發言，呼籲加快中非共和國政治程序。孫磊說，今年以來，中非共和國政治程序持續推進，和平建設取得積極進展，但在維護安全、加強治理和推動發展方面仍面臨挑戰。國際社會應支援該國政府和人民把握和平機遇，加快政治程序，開創發展新局面。孫磊說，中非共和國在推進國家各層級選舉方面取得巨大進步。中方鼓勵該國政府繼續推進包容性政治參與，推動各方堅持對話協商，妥善化解分歧，鞏固和平穩定的政治環境。當前，該國部分地區安全挑戰上升，地區衝突外溢及跨境武裝活動對邊境安全構成嚴重威脅。中方支援該國政府繼續推動落實和平協議，加快推進解武復員和重返社會程序，密切同鄰國溝通協調，共同維護邊境地區穩定。

【Nomura cuts CATL PT to 408 yuan; long-term demand intact, near-term profits pressured】Nomura cut CATL (03750.HK) target price to 408 yuan from 632 yuan and kept a Buy rating after third-quarter results missed expectations. Revenue rose 59% YoY and 12% QoQ to 165 bln yuan, broadly tracking battery shipments. Gross margin fell 5.1pp YoY and 2.5pp QoQ to 20.7%, driven by a mix shift toward higher domestic energy-storage sales. Net profit was 21.2 bln yuan, up 15% YoY but down 6% QoQ; net margin declined 4.9pp YoY and 2.4pp QoQ to 12.9%. Management attributed margin pressure to higher battery consumption taxes, reduced export VAT rebates and increased asset-impairment losses. Nomura expects global battery demand to remain resilient over the long term—supported by rising EV penetration in Europe and emerging markets and by energy-security and AI/data-center deployments driving storage demand—and sees planned global capacity expansion progressing on schedule.

Rupee Hits 6-Week High on IPO Inflows — The Indian rupee hovered around 94.6 per dollar, strengthening to a six-week high after the holiday as expected dollar inflows from Jio Platforms’ IPO and continued Reserve Bank of India intervention supported the currency. The rupee extended its winning streak to seven sessions, gaining 5.18% year-to-date, as the central bank continued selling dollars and tightened scrutiny of large overseas investments to curb capital outflows. Investors are also awaiting the release of the RBI’s October policy meeting minutes later on Wednesday for clues on further rate hikes, following its 25-basis-point increase in the repo rate to 5.50%. However, elevated oil prices remained a downside risk, as higher crude costs could widen India’s import bill and increase demand for dollars.

【2026年亞太經合組織財長會在香港舉行】 2026年10月20日至21日，第33屆亞太經合組織（APEC）財長會在中國香港特別行政區舉行。財政部部長藍佛安作為會議主席主持會議並致辭，副部長廖岷代表中方發言。2026年APEC高官會主席陳旭出席會議併發言。香港特別行政區行政長官李家超出席歡迎晚宴。來自APEC成員經濟體、APEC觀察員以及有關國際組織等共300餘名代表參會。 

會議聚焦APEC中國年“建設亞太共同體、促進共同繁榮”主題，圍繞全球和區域宏觀經濟金融形勢、支援民生髮展的財政政策、數字基礎設施融資、因地制宜促進普惠金融發展和促進APEC青年財經人才交流合作等議題進行了討論。會議審議透過《2026年APEC財長聯合宣告》和《亞太地區數字基礎設施融資政策宣告》，為亞太經濟體深化財金合作指明瞭方向。會議還形成《支援民生髮展與投資於人——推動亞太地區可持續增長的財政政策報告》《亞太地區普惠金融創新實踐案例集》等成果檔案，並同意繼續推進亞太地區青年財經人才交流與合作。 

藍佛安指出，當前國際形勢變亂交織，地緣衝突影響外溢，世界經濟增長面臨多重挑戰。APEC作為亞太地區主要經濟合作平臺，積極促進成員開放合作，加強宏觀政策協調，推動區域經濟一體化、助力亞太地區共同發展繁榮。各方應堅定落實APEC領導人非正式會議共識，堅持多邊主義，深化財金領域務實合作，為區域經濟金融穩定和長期可持續增長作出貢獻。 

藍佛安介紹了中國經濟持續向新向優向好的態勢，強調中國將堅定不移推動高質量發展，加快培育增長新動能，著力擴大國內需求，持續擴大高水平對外開放，以中國式現代化新成就，為亞太和世界發展提供新機遇。 

會前還舉行了財長閉門會、財長與亞太工商界代表工作午餐會，分別圍繞在變動的世界中推動亞太經濟增長、動員私人資本支援數字基礎設施發展等主題開展了討論，取得良好效果。 

會議期間，藍佛安應約分別與汶萊財政部主管部長劉光明、越南財政部部長吳文俊、澳大利亞國庫部部長吉姆·查默斯、新加坡交通部部長兼財政部第二部長蕭振祥等舉行雙邊會見，就全球及區域宏觀經濟形勢，中國同相關經濟體在APEC、東盟與中日韓框架下合作以及雙邊合作等議題交換意見。藍佛安還會見了香港特別行政區行政長官李家超。

Palm Oil Nears MYR 4,800 as Profit-Taking Offsets Demand — Malaysian palm oil futures edged lower towards MYR 4,800 per tonne as traders took profits after prices approached a four-week high. Sentiment was also dampened by record inventories of 3.51 million tonnes in September, while seasonal production is expected to stay elevated in October after six consecutive monthly rises. Still, losses were tempered by firmer edible oil prices on the Dalian and Chicago markets. Exports offered support, with cargo surveyors reporting a 43.2%–58.3% mom surge in palm oil shipments during the first half of October. Festival-related demand from India, a key importer, further lifted risk appetite. Meanwhile, Indonesia raised its October crude palm oil reference price to USD 1,042.15 per tonne, while rising crude oil prices enhanced palm oil’s competitiveness as a biodiesel feedstock. Traders now monitor Malaysia’s consideration of a waiver or reduction in its crude palm oil export tax, which could improve its competitiveness against Indonesian supplies.

### 2026-10-21 12:02:49  #央行 #國際

【日本央行前理事前田榮治預計央行料維持每三個月加息一次的步伐】日本央行前理事前田榮治表示，為防範通脹偏離目標的風險，日本央行可能繼續保持大約每三個月加息一次的節奏。“我預計下一次加息會在12月，之後是明年3月或4月，”前田榮治週二接受採訪時表示，“目前加息步伐已經加快至大約每三個月一次，我認為這是合適的。”他曾負責日本央行新冠疫情初期的政策應對。這一判斷大體符合市場定價。隔夜掉期市場顯示日本央行不晚於12月加息的機率約為80%。市場普遍預計，日本央行將在10月30日舉行的下一次政策會議上維持利率不變。前田表示，不完全排除日本央行未來在連續召開的貨幣政策會議上接連加息的可能性，例如日元大幅貶值時就可能出現這種情況。但他認為目前看來這種可能性不大。他說，如果日本央行連續加息，市場可能會愈發預期下一次會議繼續行動，這將帶來重大風險，因此日本央行有理由避免連續會議加息。

【Democratic groups sue Trump administration, DOJ over alleged interference in state election administration】National Democratic organizations sued President Trump and the U.S. Department of Justice on Tuesday, alleging the administration is coordinating actions intended to undermine public confidence in the upcoming U.S. midterms by pressuring states on ballot handling. The complaint says the government has asked California, Nevada and other states to pause processing ballots suspected of involving non-citizens and to set aside tens of thousands of mailed ballots until citizenship can be verified. Filed in Washington by the Democratic Senatorial Campaign Committee and Democratic Congressional Campaign Committee, the suit argues the federal data the requests rely on is "low-quality" and often erroneous. Plaintiffs ask the court to rule the government’s demands and threats violate separation of powers, infringe states’ authority over elections, and to enjoin the administration from continuing the actions.

### 2026-10-21 11:34:19  #宏觀

【天津出臺公積金新規新增提取情形】天津市住房公積金管理中心21日對外發布相關通知，新增自住住房物業費、新購自住住房裝修費和自住住房裝修翻新費提取情形，將住房公積金使用範圍進一步拓展至住房持有期間的相關支出。 根據新政策，在天津市行政區域內持有住房的，職工及其配偶每年12月31日（含當日）前可提取一次住房公積金支付物業費，每年提取金額合計不超過3000元。 對於新購自住住房，在天津市行政區域內購買住房，符合本市購房提取住房公積金條件的，職工及其配偶可在新建住房購房合同確定的交付之日起兩年內、二手住房取得住房產權之日起兩年內就該套住房提取一次住房公積金支付住房裝修費用，提取金額合計不超過每平方米1500元乘以所購住房建築面積，且不超過15萬元。 此外，對於裝修翻新需求，在天津市行政區域內持有同一套住房滿10年且近一年內未提取住房公積金用於購房款、首付款或償還住房貸款本息的，職工及其配偶可就該套住房提取一次住房公積金用於廚衛、門窗、牆面等區域性修繕改造，改善居住條件，提取金額合計不超過3萬元。 為保障職工權益，通知還明確了相關提取業務的辦理期限調整安排。其中，規定新購住房裝修費提取截止日期在2026年9月20日至2026年12月31日期間的，提取截止日期順延6個月；2026年物業費未在2026年12月31日前提取的，提取截止日期順延至2027年6月30日。 通知自2026年10月21日起施行。

【上海：到2030年基本建成亞太領先航空樞紐 實現年客貨吞吐量1.5億人次、470萬噸左右】《新時代新徵程上海國際航空樞紐戰略規劃》近日正式釋出，《規劃》由上海市政府與中國民航局共同謀劃。《規劃》總體目標是全面建成具有世界影響力的全方位門戶複合型國際航空樞紐，成為連線國內航空運輸網路與國際航空運輸通道的核心連線節點，並分三個階段推進：第一階段，到2030年基本建成亞太領先航空樞紐，完成浦東機場四期擴建工程，最佳化上海進近管制區空域結構，實現年客貨吞吐量1.5億人次，470萬噸左右。第二階段，到2035年進入世界一流航空樞紐前列，建成南通新機場，形成“兩地三場”一體化運營的國際航空樞紐體系和高效互聯的綜合交通體系，實現年客貨吞吐量2億人次540萬噸，主樞紐運營人基本建成“超級承運人”。第三階段，到本世紀中葉，全面建成具有世界影響力的全方位門戶複合型國際航空樞紐。

### 2026-10-21 11:28:46  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #WTI #Impact bullish #Brent

【Morgan Stanley warns global refined-fuel supply could remain tight through mid-2027】Morgan Stanley says even if current war-related supply disruptions ease, global refined-product markets could remain tight through mid-2027 as unusually large refinery maintenance is constraining supply. Analysts led by Martin Latz expect planned refinery outages through Q2 next year to remove about 3.4 mln b/d of capacity — roughly 2.5 times normal and larger than current reductions from Middle East and Russian refiners. Eight months of Middle East conflict and recent reciprocal attacks on energy infrastructure between Russia and Ukraine have exacerbated the shortfall. Diesel exports have dropped sharply, pushing diesel prices above crude; diesel is critical for transport, heating and industry. Morgan Stanley says many refineries delayed maintenance to chase margins but cannot defer it indefinitely, and run rates are unlikely to rebound materially by Q2 next year.

Rupiah Holds Gains as BI Rate Decision Looms — The Indonesian rupiah hovered near IDR 17,970 per dollar on Wednesday, extending strength for a third session as the US dollar remained under pressure after the dollar index fell to a three-week low. Softer US Treasury yields and easing crude oil prices reduced support for the greenback, helping lift emerging market currencies, including rupiah. Traders awaited Bank Indonesia's policy decision later today, with markets widely expecting the central bank to hold its key rate at 5.75% for a fourth straight month following 100bps of cumulative tightening earlier this year. Sentiment was also aided by receding concerns over MSCI potentially reclassifying Indonesia as a standalone market, after regulators launched measures to address concentrated share ownership and strengthen shareholder disclosure requirements. However, the upside was capped by persistent fiscal concerns and renewed cost pressures, as El Niño-related food prices accelerated and September inflation hit a three-month high.

Yen Holds Steady Amid Sluggish Export Growth — The Japanese yen was little changed around 158.2 per dollar on Wednesday, remaining within a narrow range as investors assessed data showing that exports increased less than expected in September and posted the slowest growth since April. Still, exports extended their growth streak to 13 consecutive months, supported by strong demand for semiconductors and AI-related products. On the monetary policy front, the Bank of Japan is widely expected to leave interest rates unchanged next week before implementing a rate hike in December. Earlier this week, Governor Kazuo Ueda noted that underlying inflation was nearing the central bank’s 2% target, reinforcing expectations of a more hawkish policy stance. However, the yen remained under pressure from persistent carry trades, as Japan’s relatively low interest rates continued to weigh on its appeal. The BOJ’s gradual approach to monetary tightening is also viewed as insufficient to close the interest rate gap with other major economies.

### 2026-10-21 11:03:23  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

US Natgas Prices Edge Higher — US natural gas prices edged up to $2.97 per MMBtu on Wednesday, attempting to rebound from a four-week low hit in the previous session, as improving LNG export activity and reduced output partly offset weak demand due to mild weather. Average gas flows to the nine major US LNG export terminals rose to 18.2 bcfd so far in October from 17.9 bcfd in September. Meanwhile, gas production across the Lower 48 states averaged 111.4 bcfd this month, down from record highs of 113.3 bcfd in August and September. The storage surplus also continued to shrink, with inventories as of October 9 standing 3.9% below year-earlier levels and just 1.3% above the five-year average. Meanwhile, mild temperatures are expected to persist through late October, curbing both heating and cooling demand, while expectations of a “Super El Niño” further weigh on the winter demand outlook.

【瑞銀：降寧德時代目標價至730港元，單位利潤因產品結構及退稅變動而承壓】瑞銀髮表研報指，寧德時代第三季淨利潤為212億元，同比增長15%，但環比下跌6%，較買方預期低約10%。首九個月淨利潤為645億元，僅達全年市場共識與該行預估的62%至67%，這可能導致市場對全年共識預期的下修。若排除非經常性專案，第三季淨利潤為189億元，同比增15%，但環比降10%。期內電池銷量超過260GWh，當中儲能系統(ESS)佔約25%，電動車電池佔約75%。該行指出，按此計算的單位利潤降至每千瓦時82元，創歷史新低，主要受季度產品組合波動、低增值專案增加、9月1日起對鋰離子電池徵收消費稅，以及出口增值稅退稅率由9%降至6%影響。不過，管理層對2027年行業20%至30%的增長前景仍保持信心。瑞銀相應將該公司2026至2028年淨利潤預測下調11%至14%，降至介乎900億至1310億元，目標價由820港元下調至730港元，維持“買入”評級。

New Zealand Credit Card Spending Rises 6.9% in September — Seasonally adjusted total billings in New Zealand rose 6.9% year-on-year to NZD 4.7 billion in September 2026, following an upwardly revised 3.6% increase in August. It was the seventh consecutive month of growth, driven by higher domestic billings on cards issued in New Zealand, which climbed 5.3% to NZD 4.0 billion, while overseas billings grew 3.9%. Meanwhile, total credit limits edged down to NZD 20.8 billion, while credit limit utilisation increased to 28.7% at the end of September. The weighted average effective interest rate on personal interest-bearing advances remained at 20.0% in August 2026.

【證監會商慶軍：促進北交所新三板一體化高質量發展 三方面持續發力】中國證監會公眾公司監管司一級巡視員商慶軍10月21日在2026金融街論壇年會“共建服務中小企業資本市場新生態”平行論壇上表示，證監會將深入貫徹落實黨中央、國務院決策部署，堅持穩中求進總基調，以防風險、強監管、促發展為主線，持續健全監管制度體系，促進北交所與新三板一體化高質量發展，更好服務“十五五”規劃落地和金融強國建設。商慶軍介紹，下一步將重點從三方面推進工作：一是厚植優質企業創新發展基礎。立足服務創新型中小企業定位，釋出北交所創新性評價標準，精準識別各類創新要素，明確申報預期，為處於行業前沿、具有創新能力、持續增長潛力和專業化發展優勢的創新型中小企業提供更好服務，持續最佳化上市公司結構。同時，強化新三板規範培育功能，健全准入和退出機制，最佳化分層管理，增強創新層品牌效應和融資配置功能，完善差異化資訊披露安排，鞏固北交所與新三板“層層遞進、上下聯動、頭部引領、底層助推”的發展格局，為專精特新企業提供持續接力、精準識別的資本市場服務，更好促進新質生產力發展。二是最佳化投資生態，增強市場內在韌性。長期資金是市場“壓艙石”和“穩定器”。下一步將加大投資端建設力度，統籌促進一二級市場平衡發展，健全投資和融資相協調的資本市場功能。一方面，完善北交所發行承銷制度，穩妥實施新股市值配售，提升二級市場投資者獲得感，促進新股合理定價，持續推動詢價發行案例落地，進一步提升定價彈性；另一方面，加快建設市場產品體系，加大公募基金供給力度，穩步推出北證50ETF，促進投融資兩端平衡發展。同時，完善風險監測預警和應急處置機制，切實維護市場秩序，保護投資者合法權益。三是堅持嚴監嚴管，提升上市公司質量。依託上市公司治理專項行動，督促上市中小企業提升公司治理水平；支援獨立董事、審計委員會主動、有效履職，調動機構投資者和中小股東參與公司治理積極性，強化公司內部監督與制衡。加強對大股東、實際控制人的規範約束，緊盯上市公司“關鍵少數”，對欺詐發行、財務造假、資金佔用等嚴重侵害投資者合法權益的行為及時採取行動，嚴厲打擊重大違法行為，堅決肅清違法違規亂象。（上證報）

### 2026-10-21 10:47:08  #其他

【新方法可引導合成分子形成特定三維結構】芬蘭研究人員開發出一種新方法，可透過在人工合成分子中編碼不同層級的資訊，引導其自組裝形成特定的三維結構。這一成果有望為開發智慧材料和分子尺度器件等提供新思路。 在生物體中，蛋白質等分子能夠根據其分子序列中編碼的資訊，自發形成特定的三維結構。然而如何讓人工合成分子也能如此精確地形成預定結構，一直是化學研究面臨的一大挑戰。 芬蘭于韋斯屈萊大學研究人員開發出一種分級的序列控制方法，透過在分子中編碼兩級資訊，分步控制其自組裝過程。第一級資訊進行初步選擇，將分子的可能結構限定為4種型別的螺旋。第二級資訊進一步引導在這4種螺旋中形成一種特定螺旋：逆時針三螺旋，即三條分子鏈相互纏繞、逆時針盤旋。與此同時，這種分級控制還會有效抑制其他可能結構的形成。 研究表明，透過對分子序列進行精細調整，可以精確控制分子最終形成的三維結構。研究團隊希望進一步提高對分子自組裝過程的預測和調控能力，使人工合成分子能夠按照預先設計的方式，形成更加複雜的三維結構。 相關研究成果已發表在學術期刊《應用化學國際版》上。(新華社)

---

Indonesian Shares Retreat Ahead of BI Rate Call — Indonesian equities fell 84 points, or 1.6%, to 5,197 on Wednesday morning, reversing a prior rally after regulatory measures aimed at regaining investor confidence, including adjustments to stocks with high shareholding concentration and greater disclosure of shareholder affiliations. Traders turned cautious ahead of Bank Indonesia's rate decision later today. While the central bank is expected to hold borrowing costs for a fourth month, room for easing is limited after a cumulative 100bps hike in May and June. Still, the decline was capped by record FDI in Q3 2026, lifted by solid inflows into the base metal industry. In top trading partner China, key lending rates were left unchanged for a 17th month after stronger-than-expected Q3 GDP. Cyclicals, basic materials, and financials were mainly lower, partly offset by strength in infrastructure, non-cyclicals, and transport. Notable decliners included Solusi Energi Digital (-6.9%), Mitra Adiperkasa (-6.0%), and Medco Energi (-3.9%).

【我國加強頂層設計 努力降低氣候變化風險和損害】在國務院新聞辦公室今天（21日）上午舉行的新聞釋出會上，生態環境部副部長李高介紹，生態環境部正在會同有關部門進一步加強頂層設計，努力降低氣候變化風險和損害。近年來氣候變化影響持續加劇，極端災害造成重大人員傷亡和財產損失。氣候變化正在從未來的風險隱患演變為現實緊迫的危機。我國是氣候變化敏感區和影響顯著區，氣候變化背景下各種極端災害事件對我國糧食生產、生態安全、基礎設施、城市執行和人民生命健康等造成嚴重威脅。李高介紹，我國堅持減緩和適應並重原則，加強氣候變化監測預警和風險管理，提升重點領域適應氣候變化能力，推動將適應氣候變化納入經濟社會發展的各領域各方面，提高脆弱人群的風險防範和健康適應能力。全國31個省（區、市）已印發適應行動方案，公共衛生與健康領域釋出《國家氣候變化健康適應行動方案》。生態環境部已指導39個城市深化氣候適應型城市建設試點，形成了不少好經驗好做法，比如將適應氣候變化融入城市規劃佈局、開展定性定量相結合的氣候變化風險評估、利用人工智慧提升預報預警能力等。深圳、海口、成都等試點城市積極探索建設氣候適應型社群，取得良好效果。

Taiwan Stocks Rebound from Near 5-Week Low — The TAIEX, Taiwan’s benchmark stock index, advanced 860 points, or 1.9%, to 47,153 in Wednesday morning trade, erasing losses from the previous two sessions and tracking gains on Wall Street overnight amid easing Treasury yields and oil prices. Electronic technology rebounded, rising 1.5% after falling 1.0% in the previous session due to concerns over AI investment. Moderating oil prices lifted sentiment, easing concerns about inflation risks and interest rate hikes. 【黃河干流寧夏段連續9年保持Ⅱ類水質】記者20日從寧夏生態環境廳獲悉，“十四五”以來，寧夏不斷加強水生態環境保護與修復，全區水生態環境質量持續穩定向好。截至2025年底，黃河干流寧夏段已連續9年穩定保持Ⅱ類水質，地表水國控斷面Ⅲ類及以上優良水質比例達到85%，國家監測的15個城市集中式飲用水水源地水質全部達標並保持優良。 近年來，寧夏強化重點斷面水質管控，實時監測水質變化，採取針對性防控措施，確保水質穩定達標。持續開展排汙口排查整治，累計排查各類排汙口4682個，逐項整改銷號，整治率達到97.1%。透過實施重點入黃排水溝生態治理工程，全區22條重點入黃排水溝水質全部達到或優於地表水Ⅳ類標準。全面推行河湖長制以來，寧夏累計規範整治236處黃河寧夏段“亂佔、亂建”問題。 另外，寧夏推進汙水處理提質增效，切實鞏固黑臭水體治理成效。目前，全區已建成城市生活汙水處理廠全部達到一級A排放標準，城市生活汙水處理率、收集率分別達到99.15%、79.8%。全區地級城市建成區13條黑臭水體已全部消除，未出現返黑返臭；12個縣城建成區排查未發現黑臭水體。(新華社)

【壁仞科技魏明：算力閒置的另一面，是高階算力“非常缺”】2026可持續全球領導者大會於10月20日-22日在上海黃浦世博園區舉辦。在《以東方力量推進高質量發展》行業主題論壇上，壁仞科技戰略規劃高階副總裁魏明指出，算力市場呈現明顯的結構性矛盾：一方面部分算力處於閒置狀態，另一方面高階算力卻“非常缺”。“如果問大模型公司今年最挑戰的事情是什麼，那就是缺算力，缺高階算力。”魏明表示。他介紹，目前國產高階晶片的算力已經起來，能夠提供相應服務，這為緩解高階算力緊缺提供了現實支撐。與此同時，閒置算力也可透過PD分離等方式盤活——把算力較強的國產卡與其他視訊記憶體較強卡配合使用，讓不同晶片各展所長，以新技術逐步化解閒置問題。在魏明看來，隨著國產晶片能力提升與排程技術不斷成熟，閒置與緊缺並存的矛盾有望逐步緩解，他對國產卡的應用前景充滿信心。

### 2026-10-21 10:10:57  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

European Natural Gas Rebounds — European natural gas prices rose to €87/MWh on Wednesday, moving back near a multi-year high after sliding more than 3% in the previous session as traders weighed mixed reports on efforts to end the Middle East war. Tuesday’s decline came after unconfirmed reports that Qatar and Pakistan had submitted a new proposal aimed at reviving talks to end the conflict. However, a diplomat involved in the negotiations said reports of an imminent agreement were incorrect. European gas prices remain more than double their pre-conflict levels amid ongoing disruptions to LNG supplies from the Persian Gulf and unusually low inventories. As of October 20, European storage facilities were 74.62% full, the lowest seasonal level since records began in 2009. The situation is particularly concerning in Germany, Europe’s largest economy, with storage levels just above 61%. Strong LNG demand from Asia also increases the risk of more intense competition for cargoes when colder weather arrives.
